# Effects for determining the Chinese year/zodiac/element/etc. when THIS was born

set_this_chinese_year_zodiac_element_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_character_flag = chinese_zodiac_set
			}
		}
		
		set_character_flag = chinese_zodiac_set
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				year_of_birth >= 1400
			}
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					year_of_birth == 1500
				}

					limit = {
						OR = {
							month_of_birth == 0
							AND = {

					}
				}
				
				else = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								month_of_birth == 0
								AND = {
									month_of_birth == 1
									day_of_birth < 10
								}
							}
						}
						
						add_trait = element_earth
						add_trait = zodiac_rabbit
					}
					
					else = {
						add_trait = element_metal
						add_trait = zodiac_dragon
					}
				}
			}
			
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					year_of_birth >= 10
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						year_of_birth == 19
					}
					
					if = {
						limit = {
							month_of_birth == 0
							day_of_birth < 23
						}
						
						add_trait = element_earth
						add_trait = zodiac_tiger
					}
					
					else = {
						add_trait = element_earth
						add_trait = zodiac_rabbit
					}
				}
				
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						year_of_birth == 18
					}
					
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								month_of_birth == 0
								AND = {
									month_of_birth == 1
									day_of_birth < 3
								}
							}
						}
						
						add_trait = element_fire
						add_trait = zodiac_ox
					}
					
					else = {
						add_trait = element_earth
						add_trait = zodiac_tiger
					}
				}
				
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						year_of_birth == 17
					}
					
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								month_of_birth == 0
								AND = {
									month_of_birth == 1
									day_of_birth < 13
								}
							}
						}
						
						add_trait = element_fire
						add_trait = zodiac_rat
					}
					
					else = {
						add_trait = element_fire
						add_trait = zodiac_ox
					}
				}
				
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						year_of_birth == 16
					}
					
					if = {
						limit = {
							month_of_birth == 0
							day_of_birth < 26
						}
						
						add_trait = element_wood
						add_trait = zodiac_pig
					}
					
					else = {
						add_trait = element_fire
						add_trait = zodiac_rat
					}
				}
				
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						year_of_birth == 15
					}
					
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								month_of_birth == 0
								AND = {
									month_of_birth == 1
									day_of_birth < 6
								}
							}
						}
						
						add_trait = element_wood
						add_trait = zodiac_dog
					}
					
					else = {
						add_trait = element_wood
						add_trait = zodiac_pig
					}
				}
				
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						year_of_birth == 14
					}
					
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								month_of_birth == 0
								AND = {
									month_of_birth == 1
									day_of_birth < 16
								}
							}
						}
						
						add_trait = element_water
						add_trait = zodiac_rooster
					}
					
					else = {
						add_trait = element_wood
						add_trait = zodiac_dog
					}
				}
				
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						year_of_birth == 13
					}
					
					if = {
						limit = {
							month_of_birth == 0
							day_of_birth < 28
						}
						
						add_trait = element_water
						add_trait = zodiac_monkey
					}
					
					else = {
						add_trait = element_water
						add_trait = zodiac_rooster
					}
				}
				
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						year_of_birth == 12
					}
					
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								month_of_birth == 0
								AND = {
									month_of_birth == 1
									day_of_birth < 9
								}
							}
						}
						
						add_trait = element_metal
						add_trait = zodiac_goat
					}
					
					else = {
						add_trait = element_water
						add_trait = zodiac_monkey
					}
				}
				
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						year_of_birth == 11
					}
					
					if = {
						limit = {
							month_of_birth == 0
							day_of_birth < 21
						}
						
						add_trait = element_metal
						add_trait = zodiac_horse
					}
					
					else = {
						add_trait = element_metal
						add_trait = zodiac_goat
					}
				}
				
				else = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							month_of_birth == 0
						}
						
						add_trait = element_earth
						add_trait = zodiac_snake
					}
					
					else = {
						add_trait = element_metal
						add_trait = zodiac_horse
					}
				}
			}
			
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						year_of_birth == 9
					}
					
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								month_of_birth == 0
								AND = {
									month_of_birth == 1
									day_of_birth < 12
								}
							}
						}
						
						add_trait = element_earth
						add_trait = zodiac_dragon
					}
					
					else = {
						add_trait = element_earth
						add_trait = zodiac_snake
					}
				}
				
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						year_of_birth == 8
					}
					
					if = {
						limit = {
							month_of_birth == 0
							day_of_birth < 25
						}
						
						add_trait = element_fire
						add_trait = zodiac_rabbit
					}
					
					else = {
						add_trait = element_earth
						add_trait = zodiac_dragon
					}
				}
				
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						year_of_birth == 7
					}
					
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								month_of_birth == 0
								AND = {
									month_of_birth == 1
									day_of_birth < 4
								}
							}
						}
						
						add_trait = element_fire
						add_trait = zodiac_tiger
					}
					
					else = {
						add_trait = element_fire
						add_trait = zodiac_rabbit
					}
				}
				
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						year_of_birth == 6
					}
					
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								month_of_birth == 0
								AND = {
									month_of_birth == 1
									day_of_birth < 15
								}
							}
						}
						
						add_trait = element_wood
						add_trait = zodiac_ox
					}
					
					else = {
						add_trait = element_fire
						add_trait = zodiac_tiger
					}
				}
				
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						year_of_birth == 5
					}
					
					if = {
						limit = {
							month_of_birth == 0
							day_of_birth < 27
						}
						
						add_trait = element_wood
						add_trait = zodiac_rat
					}
					
					else = {
						add_trait = element_wood
						add_trait = zodiac_ox
					}
				}
				
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						year_of_birth == 4
					}
					
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								month_of_birth == 0
								AND = {
									month_of_birth == 1
									day_of_birth < 7
								}
							}
						}
						
						add_trait = element_water
						add_trait = zodiac_pig
					}
					
					else = {
						add_trait = element_wood
						add_trait = zodiac_rat
					}
				}
				
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						year_of_birth == 3
					}
					
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								month_of_birth == 0
								AND = {
									month_of_birth == 1
									day_of_birth < 18
								}
							}
						}
						
						add_trait = element_water
						add_trait = zodiac_dog
					}
					
					else = {
						add_trait = element_water
						add_trait = zodiac_pig
					}
				}
				
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						year_of_birth == 2
					}
					
					if = {
						limit = {
							month_of_birth == 0
							day_of_birth < 30
						}
						
						add_trait = element_metal
						add_trait = zodiac_rooster
					}
					
					else = {
						add_trait = element_water
						add_trait = zodiac_dog
					}
				}
				
				else = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								month_of_birth == 0
								AND = {
									month_of_birth == 1
									day_of_birth < 10
								}
							}
						}
						
						add_trait = element_metal
						add_trait = zodiac_monkey
					}
					
					else = {
						add_trait = element_metal
						add_trait = zodiac_rooster
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	if = {
		limit = {
			trait = zodiac_rooster
		}
		add_zodiac_rooster_attraction_trait_effect = yes
	}
}

# Effects for determining the zodiac/element for the OLD year

set_old_chinese_year_effect = {
	set_variable = {
		which = old_chinese_year
		value = 0
	}
	
	# To avoid having to use the entire sixty year cycle, we'll assume that the Lunar New Year takes place February 1 every year
	export_to_variable = {
		which = old_chinese_year
		value = year
		who = ROOT
	}
	
	change_variable = {
		which = old_chinese_year
		value = -1 # We are interested in the PREVIOUS year!
	}
	
	change_variable = {
		which = old_chinese_year
		value = 2697
	}
}

set_old_chinese_zodiac_effect = {
	# This assumes we've already used set_old_chinese_year_effect
	set_variable = {
		which = old_chinese_zodiac
		which = old_chinese_year
	}
	
	modulo_variable = {
		which = old_chinese_zodiac
		value = 12
	}
	
	clr_global_flag = lunar_old_year_zodiac_is_rat
	clr_global_flag = lunar_old_year_zodiac_is_ox
	clr_global_flag = lunar_old_year_zodiac_is_tiger
	clr_global_flag = lunar_old_year_zodiac_is_rabbit
	clr_global_flag = lunar_old_year_zodiac_is_dragon
	clr_global_flag = lunar_old_year_zodiac_is_snake
	clr_global_flag = lunar_old_year_zodiac_is_horse
	clr_global_flag = lunar_old_year_zodiac_is_goat
	clr_global_flag = lunar_old_year_zodiac_is_monkey
	clr_global_flag = lunar_old_year_zodiac_is_roster
	clr_global_flag = lunar_old_year_zodiac_is_dog
	clr_global_flag = lunar_old_year_zodiac_is_pig
	
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_variable_equal = { which = old_chinese_zodiac value = 1 }
		}
		set_global_flag = lunar_old_year_zodiac_is_rat
	}
	
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			is_variable_equal = { which = old_chinese_zodiac value = 2 }
		}
		set_global_flag = lunar_old_year_zodiac_is_ox
	}
	
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			is_variable_equal = { which = old_chinese_zodiac value = 3 }
		}
		set_global_flag = lunar_old_year_zodiac_is_tiger
	}
	
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			is_variable_equal = { which = old_chinese_zodiac value = 4 }
		}
		set_global_flag = lunar_old_year_zodiac_is_rabbit
	}
	
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			is_variable_equal = { which = old_chinese_zodiac value = 5 }
		}
		set_global_flag = lunar_old_year_zodiac_is_dragon
	}
	
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			is_variable_equal = { which = old_chinese_zodiac value = 6 }
		}
		set_global_flag = lunar_old_year_zodiac_is_snake
	}
	
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			is_variable_equal = { which = old_chinese_zodiac value = 7 }
		}
		set_global_flag = lunar_old_year_zodiac_is_horse
	}
	
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			is_variable_equal = { which = old_chinese_zodiac value = 8 }
		}
		set_global_flag = lunar_old_year_zodiac_is_goat
	}
	
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			is_variable_equal = { which = old_chinese_zodiac value = 9 }
		}
		set_global_flag = lunar_old_year_zodiac_is_monkey
	}
	
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			is_variable_equal = { which = old_chinese_zodiac value = 10 }
		}
		set_global_flag = lunar_old_year_zodiac_is_rooster
	}
	
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			is_variable_equal = { which = old_chinese_zodiac value = 11 }
		}
		set_global_flag = lunar_old_year_zodiac_is_dog
	}
	
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			is_variable_equal = { which = old_chinese_zodiac value = 0 }
		}
		set_global_flag = lunar_old_year_zodiac_is_pig
	}
}

set_old_chinese_element_effect = {
	# This assumes we've already used set_old_chinese_year_effect
	set_variable = {
		which = old_chinese_element
		which = old_chinese_year
	}
	
	modulo_variable = {
		which = old_chinese_element
		value = 10
	}
	
	clr_global_flag = lunar_old_year_element_is_wood
	clr_global_flag = lunar_old_year_element_is_fire
	clr_global_flag = lunar_old_year_element_is_earth
	clr_global_flag = lunar_old_year_element_is_metal
	clr_global_flag = lunar_old_year_element_is_water
	
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				which = old_chinese_element
				value > 0
			}
			check_variable = {
				which = old_chinese_element
				value < 3
			}
		}
		set_global_flag = lunar_old_year_element_is_wood
	}
	
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				which = old_chinese_element
				value > 2
			}
			check_variable = {
				which = old_chinese_element
				value < 5
			}
		}
		set_global_flag = lunar_old_year_element_is_fire
	}
	
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				which = old_chinese_element
				value > 4
			}
			check_variable = {
				which = old_chinese_element
				value < 7
			}
		}
		set_global_flag = lunar_old_year_element_is_earth
	}
	
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				which = old_chinese_element
				value > 6
			}
			check_variable = {
				which = old_chinese_element
				value < 9
			}
		}
		set_global_flag = lunar_old_year_element_is_metal
	}
	
	else = {
		set_global_flag = lunar_old_year_element_is_water
	}
}

set_old_chinese_year_zodiac_element_effect = {
	set_old_chinese_year_effect = yes
	set_old_chinese_zodiac_effect = yes
	set_old_chinese_element_effect = yes
}
